Rows\n//   are distributed such that every thread gets a (nearly) equal share of the\n//   non-zero elements, which is what determines the amount of work / memory\n//   traffic in SpMV. The partitioning is a pure function of the row delimiters\n//   and the team size, so all parallel regions agree on it.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows\n//   dim:           number of rows in the matrix\n//   tid:           thread id\n//   nthreads:      size of the thread team\n//   begin/end:     output - row range assigned to this thread\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nstatic inline void rowRangeForThread(const index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t dim, const int tid,\n                                     const int nthreads, index_t& begin, index_t& end) {\n    const index_t nnz = rowDelimiters[dim];\n    const auto split = [&](const int t) -> index_t {\n        if (t == 0) return 0;\n        if (t >= nthreads) return dim;\n        // First row whose starting offset reaches this thread's share of non-zeroes\n        const index_t target = static_cast<index_t>((static_cast<uint64_t>(nnz) * static_cast<uint64_t>(t)) /\n                                                    static_cast<uint64_t>(nthreads));\n        const index_t* pos = std::lower_bound(rowDelimiters, rowDelimiters + dim + 1, target);\n        return static_cast<index_t>(pos - rowDelimiters);\n    };\n    begin = split(tid);\n    end = split(tid + 1);\n    if (end < begin) end = begin;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: fill\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Initialize array with random values\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   A: pointer to the array to initialize\n//   n: number of elements in the array\n//   maxVal: specifies range of random values [0, maxVal]\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            const auto col = cols[j];\n            t += val[j] * vec[col];\n        }\n        out[i] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            // For very small values, check absolute error\n            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", i, ref, res);\n                return false;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, ref, res, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    index_t numRows = 1024;\n    index_t sparsity = 10;\n    index_t iterations = 10;\n    double maxVal = 1.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-m\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Calculate number of non-zero elements\n    const index_t nItems = (numRows * numRows) / sparsity;\n\n    printf(\"Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Matrix size: %u x %u\\n\", numRows, numRows);\n    printf(\"Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\\n\", sparsity);\n    printf(\"Non-zero elements: %u (%.2f%% sparse)\\n\", \n           nItems, 100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(nItems) / (numRows * numRows)));\n    printf(\"Iterations: %u\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Max value: %.2f\\n\", maxVal);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Allocate and initialize data structures\n    std::vector<double> h_val(nItems);                  // Non-zero values\n    std::vector<index_t> h_cols(nItems);                // Column indices\n    std::vector<index_t> h_rowDelimiters(numRows + 1);  // Row delimiters\n    std::vector<double> h_vec(numRows);                 // Dense vector\n    std::vector<double> h_out(numRows);                 // Output vector\n\n    printf(\"Initializing data structures...\\n\");\n    fill(h_vec.data(), numRows, maxVal);\n    fill(h_val.data(), nItems, maxVal);\n    initRandomMatrix(h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), nItems, numRows);\n\n    // For validation, compute reference solution\n    std::vector<double> h_reference;\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Computing reference solution...\\n\");\n        h_reference.resize(numRows);\n        spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), \n                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_reference.data());\n    }\n\n    // Perform SpMV computation\n    printf(\"Computing SpMV...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    for (index_t iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n        spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(),\n                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_out.data());\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n    \n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    const double gflops = (2.0 * nItems * iterations) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    const double avgTime = duration.count() / static_cast<double>(iterations);\n    \n    printf(\"Average time per iteration: %.3f ms\\n\", avgTime);\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(h_out, \"OutputVector\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        const bool valid = verifyResults(h_reference.data(), h_out.data(), numRows);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":138,"oldLines":13,"newStart":138,"newLines":21,"lines":[" // ****************************************************************************"," void spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,","              const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {","-    for (index_t i = 0; Fixed once at startup so that\n// the row partitioning used for NUMA-aware first touch matches the one used during\n// the actual computation.\nstatic int gNumThreads = 1;\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: rowRangeForThread\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes the range of rows [begin, end) a thread is responsible for. Rows\n//   are distributed such that every thread gets a (nearly) equal share of the\n//   non-zero elements, which is what determines the amount of work / memory\n//   traffic in SpMV. The partitioning is a pure function of the row delimiters\n//   and the team size, so all parallel regions agree on it.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows\n//   dim:           number of rows in the matrix\n//   tid:           thread id\n//   nthreads:      size of the thread team\n//   begin/end:     output - row range assigned to this thread\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nstatic inline void rowRangeForThread(const index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t dim, const int tid,\n                                     const int nthreads, index_t& begin, index_t& end) {\n    const index_t nnz = rowDelimiters[dim];\n    const auto split = [&](const int t) -> index_t {\n        if (t == 0) return 0;\n        if (t >= nthreads) return dim;\n        // First row whose starting offset reaches this thread's share of non-zeroes\n        const index_t target = static_cast<index_t>((static_cast<uint64_t>(nnz) * static_cast<uint64_t>(t)) /\n                                                    static_cast<uint64_t>(nthreads));\n        const index_t* pos = std::lower_bound(rowDelimiters, rowDelimiters + dim + 1, target);\n        return static_cast<index_t>(pos - rowDelimiters);\n    };\n    begin = split(tid);\n    end = split(tid + 1);\n    if (end < begin) end = begin;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: fill\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Initialize array with random values\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   A: pointer to the array to initialize\n//   n: number of elements in the array\n//   maxVal: specifies range of random values [0, maxVal]\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n#pragma omp parallel num_threads(gNumThreads)\n    {\n        index_t rowBegin, rowEnd;\n        rowRangeForThread(rowDelimiters, dim, omp_get_thread_num(), omp_get_num_threads(), rowBegin, rowEnd);\n\n        // The accumulation order within a row is kept identical to the serial\n        // version, so results are bit-for-bit reproducible.\n        for (index_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {\n            double t = 0.0;\n            for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n                const auto col = cols[j];\n                t += val[j] * vec[col];\n            }\n            out[i] = t;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            // For very small values, check absolute error\n            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", i, ref, res);\n                return false;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, ref, res, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    index_t numRows = 1024;\n    index_t sparsity = 10;\n    index_t iterations = 10;\n    double maxVal = 1.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-m\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Calculate number of non-zero elements\n    const index_t nItems = (numRows * numRows) / sparsity;\n\n    printf(\"Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Matrix size: %u x %u\\n\", numRows, numRows);\n    printf(\"Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\\n\", sparsity);\n    printf(\"Non-zero elements: %u (%.2f%% sparse)\\n\", \n           nItems, 100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(nItems) / (numRows * numRows)));\n    printf(\"Iterations: %u\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Max value: %.2f\\n\", maxVal);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Allocate and initialize data structures\n    std::vector<double> h_val(nItems); The partitioning is a pure function of the row delimiters\n//   and the team size, so all parallel regions agree on it.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows\n//   dim:           number of rows in the matrix\n//   tid:           thread id\n//   nthreads:      size of the thread team\n//   begin/end:     output - row range assigned to this thread\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nstatic inline void rowRangeForThread(const index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t dim, const int tid,\n                                     const int nthreads, index_t& begin, index_t& end) {\n    const index_t nnz = rowDelimiters[dim];\n    const auto split = [&](const int t) -> index_t {\n        if (t == 0) return 0;\n        if (t >= nthreads) return dim;\n        // First row whose starting offset reaches this thread's share of non-zeroes\n        const index_t target = static_cast<index_t>((static_cast<uint64_t>(nnz) * static_cast<uint64_t>(t)) /\n                                                    static_cast<uint64_t>(nthreads));\n        const index_t* pos = std::lower_bound(rowDelimiters, rowDelimiters + dim + 1, target);\n        return static_cast<index_t>(pos - rowDelimiters);\n    };\n    begin = split(tid);\n    end = split(tid + 1);\n    if (end < begin) end = begin;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: fill\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Initialize array with random values\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   A: pointer to the array to initialize\n//   n: number of elements in the array\n//   maxVal: specifies range of random values [0, maxVal]\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n#pragma omp parallel num_threads(gNumThreads)\n    {\n        index_t rowBegin, rowEnd;\n        rowRangeForThread(rowDelimiters, dim, omp_get_thread_num(), omp_get_num_threads(), rowBegin, rowEnd);\n\n        // The accumulation order within a row is kept identical to the serial\n        // version, so results are bit-for-bit reproducible.\n        for (index_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {\n            double t = 0.0;\n            for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n                const auto col = cols[j];\n                t += val[j] * vec[col];\n            }\n            out[i] = t;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            // For very small values, check absolute error\n            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", i, ref, res);\n                return false;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, ref, res, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n#pragma omp parallel num_threads(gNumThreads)\n    {\n        index_t rowBegin, rowEnd;\n        rowRangeForThread(rowDelimiters, dim, omp_get_thread_num(), omp_get_num_threads(), rowBegin, rowEnd);\n\n        // The accumulation order within a row is kept identical to the serial\n        // version, so results are bit-for-bit reproducible.\n        for (index_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {\n            double t = 0.0;\n            for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n                const auto col = cols[j];\n                t += val[j] * vec[col];\n            }\n            out[i] = t;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            // For very small values, check absolute error\n            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", i, ref, res);\n                return false;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, ref, res, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    index_t numRows = 1024;\n    index_t sparsity = 10;\n    index_t iterations = 10;\n    double maxVal = 1.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-m\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Calculate number of non-zero elements\n    const index_t nItems = (numRows * numRows) / sparsity;\n\n    printf(\"Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Matrix size: %u x %u\\n\", numRows, numRows);\n    printf(\"Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\\n\", sparsity);\n    printf(\"Non-zero elements: %u (%.2f%% sparse)\\n\", \n           nItems, 100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(nItems) / (numRows * numRows)));\n    printf(\"Iterations: %u\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Max value: %.2f\\n\", maxVal);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    gNumThreads = omp_get_max_threads();\n    printf(\"OpenMP threads: %d\\n\", gNumThreads);\n\n    // Allocate and initialize data structures\n    std::vector<double> h_val(nItems);                  // Non-zero values\n    std::vector<index_t> h_cols(nItems);                // Column indices\n    std::vector<index_t> h_rowDelimiters(numRows + 1);  // Row delimiters\n    std::vector<double> h_vec(numRows);                 // Dense vector\n    std::vector<double> h_out(numRows);                 // Output vector\n\n    printf(\"Initializing data structures...\\n\");\n    fill(h_vec.data(), numRows, maxVal);\n    fill(h_val.data(), nItems, maxVal);\n    initRandomMatrix(h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), nItems, numRows);\n\n    // Move the working set into memory that is first touched by the thread which\n    // will later operate on it, so that the pages end up on the correct NUMA node.\n    const auto allocLocal = [](const size_t bytes) -> void* {\n        const size_t rounded = ((bytes + 63) / 64) * 64;\n        void* p = std::aligned_alloc(64, rounded);\n        if (p == nullptr) {\n            printf(\"Allocation of %zu bytes failed\\n\", rounded);\n            exit(1);\n        }\n        return p;\n    };\n\n    auto* d_val = static_cast<double*>(allocLocal(sizeof(double) * nItems));\n    auto* d_cols = static_cast<index_t*>(allocLocal(sizeof(index_t) * nItems));\n    auto* d_rowDelimiters = static_cast<index_t*>(allocLocal(sizeof(index_t) * (numRows + 1)));\n    auto* d_vec = static_cast<double*>(allocLocal(sizeof(double) * numRows));\n    auto* d_out = static_cast<double*>(allocLocal(sizeof(double) * numRows));\n\n#pragma omp parallel num_threads(gNumThreads)\n    {\n        index_t rowBegin, rowEnd;\n        rowRangeForThread(h_rowDelimiters.data(), numRows, omp_get_thread_num(), omp_get_num_threads(),\n                          rowBegin, rowEnd);\n\n        for (index_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {\n            d_rowDelimiters[i] = h_rowDelimiters[i];\n            d_vec[i] = h_vec[i];\n            d_out[i] = 0.0;\n        }\n        for (index_t j = h_rowDelimiters[rowBegin]; j < h_rowDelimiters[rowEnd]; ++j) {\n            d_val[j] = h_val[j];\n            d_cols[j] = h_cols[j];\n        }\n    }\n    d_rowDelimiters[numRows] = nItems;\n\n    // For validation, compute reference solution\n    std::vector<double> h_reference;\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Computing reference solution...\\n\");\n        h_reference.resize(numRows);\n        spmvCpu(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, numRows, h_reference.data());\n    }\n\n    // Perform SpMV computation\n    printf(\"Computing SpMV...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    for (index_t iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n        spmvCpu(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, numRows, d_out);\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    std::copy(d_out, d_out + numRows, h_out.begin());\n    std::free(d_val);\n    std::free(d_cols);\n    std::free(d_rowDelimiters);\n    std::free(d_vec);\n    std::free(d_out);\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n    \n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    const double gflops = (2.0 * nItems * iterations) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    const double avgTime = duration.count() / static_cast<double>(iterations);\n    \n    printf(\"Average time per iteration: %.3f ms\\n\", avgTime);\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(h_out, \"OutputVector\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        const bool valid = verifyResults(h_reference.data(), h_out.data(), numRows);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":265,"oldLines":7,"newStart":265,"newLines":7,"lines":["     // Move c < CPU_SETSIZE; ++c) {\n        if (CPU_ISSET(c, &allowedSet)) allowed.push_back(c);\n    }\n    // Do not interfere when the process is oversubscribed\n    if (allowed.empty() || nthreads > static_cast<int>(allowed.size())) return;\n\n    // NUMA node of every allowed CPU (0 if the topology cannot be read)\n    std::vector<int> nodeOf(CPU_SETSIZE, 0);\n    int numNodes = 0;\n    for (int n = 0; Fixed once at startup so that\n// the row partitioning used for NUMA-aware first touch matches the one used during\n// the actual computation.\nstatic int gNumThreads = 1;\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: rowRangeForThread\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes the range of rows [begin, end) a thread is responsible for. Rows\n//   are distributed such that every thread gets a (nearly) equal share of the\n//   non-zero elements, which is what determines the amount of work / memory\n//   traffic in SpMV. The partitioning is a pure function of the row delimiters\n//   and the team size, so all parallel regions agree on it.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows\n//   dim:           number of rows in the matrix\n//   tid:           thread id\n//   nthreads:      size of the thread team\n//   begin/end:     output - row range assigned to this thread\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nstatic inline void rowRangeForThread(const index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t dim, const int tid,\n                                     const int nthreads, index_t& begin, index_t& end) {\n    const index_t nnz = rowDelimiters[dim];\n    const auto split = [&](const int t) -> index_t {\n        if (t == 0) return 0;\n        if (t >= nthreads) return dim;\n        // First row whose starting offset reaches this thread's share of non-zeroes\n        const index_t target = static_cast<index_t>((static_cast<uint64_t>(nnz) * static_cast<uint64_t>(t)) /\n                                                    static_cast<uint64_t>(nthreads));\n        const index_t* pos = std::lower_bound(rowDelimiters, rowDelimiters + dim + 1, target);\n        return static_cast<index_t>(pos - rowDelimiters);\n    };\n    begin = split(tid);\n    end = split(tid + 1);\n    if (end < begin) end = begin;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: fill\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Initialize array with random values\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   A: pointer to the array to initialize\n//   n: number of elements in the array\n//   maxVal: specifies range of random values [0, maxVal]\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n#pragma omp parallel num_threads(gNumThreads)\n    {\n        index_t rowBegin, rowEnd;\n        rowRangeForThread(rowDelimiters, dim, omp_get_thread_num(), omp_get_num_threads(), rowBegin, rowEnd);\n\n        // The accumulation order within a row is kept identical to the serial\n        // version, so results are bit-for-bit reproducible.\n        for (index_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {\n            double t = 0.0;\n            for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n                const auto col = cols[j];\n                t += val[j] * vec[col];\n            }\n            out[i] = t;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Fixed once at startup so that\n// the row partitioning used for NUMA-aware first touch matches the one used during\n// the actual computation.\nstatic int gNumThreads = 1;\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: parseCpuList\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Parses a sysfs CPU list of the form \"0-63,128-191\" into the given vector.\n//   Returns false if the file could not be read.\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nstatic bool parseCpuList(const char* path, std::vector<int>& cpus) {\n    FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\");\n    if (f == nullptr) return false;\n    char buf[4096];\n    const bool ok = fgets(buf, sizeof(buf), f) != nullptr;\n    fclose(f);\n    if (!ok) return false;\n\n    const char* p = buf;\n    while (*p != '\\0') {\n        char* endp = nullptr;\n        const long first = strtol(p, &endp, 10);\n        if (endp == p) break;\n        p = endp;\n        long last = first;\n        if (*p == '-') {\n            last = strtol(p + 1, &endp, 10);\n            p = endp;\n        }\n        for (long c = first; c <= last; ++c) {\n            cpus.push_back(static_cast<int>(c));\n        }\n        if (*p == ',') ++p; else break;\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: pinThreads\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   SpMV is memory bound, so both bandwidth and the NUMA-aware data placement\n//   done by first touch depend on threads staying on the core they started on.\n//   If the user did not request an OpenMP binding policy, threads are pinned\n//   here: cores are handed out round robin over the NUMA nodes (to use the\n//   bandwidth of all memory controllers) and SMT siblings are only used once\n//   every physical core is taken.\n//\n//   Any explicit OMP_PROC_BIND setting is left untouched.\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nstatic void pinThreads(const int nthreads) {\n    if (omp_get_proc_bind() != omp_proc_bind_false) return;\n\n    cpu_set_t allowedSet;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowedSet);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowedSet), &allowedSet) != 0) return;\n\n    std::vector<int> allowed;\n    for (int c = 0; c < CPU_SETSIZE; ++c) {\n        if (CPU_ISSET(c, &allowedSet)) allowed.push_back(c);\n    }\n    // Do not interfere when the process is oversubscribed\n    if (allowed.empty() || nthreads > static_cast<int>(allowed.size())) return;\n\n    // NUMA node of every allowed CPU (0 if the topology cannot be read)\n    std::vector<int> nodeOf(CPU_SETSIZE, 0);\n    int numNodes = 0;\n    for (int n = 0; n < 1024; ++n) {\n        char path[256];\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/node/node%d/cpulist\", n);\n        std::vector<int> nodeCpus;\n        if (!parseCpuList(path, nodeCpus)) break;\n        for (const int c : nodeCpus) {\n            if (c >= 0 && c < CPU_SETSIZE) nodeOf[c] = n;\n        }\n        numNodes = n + 1;\n    }\n    if (numNodes == 0) numNodes = 1;\n\n    // SMT rank of every allowed CPU: its position within the sibling list\n    int maxRank = 0;\n    std::vector<int> smtRankOf(CPU_SETSIZE, 0);\n    for (const int c : allowed) {\n        char path[256];\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/thread_siblings_list\", c);\n        std::vector<int> siblings;\n        if (!parseCpuList(path, siblings)) continue;\n        std::sort(siblings.begin(), siblings.end());\n        const auto it = std::find(siblings.begin(), siblings.end(), c);\n        if (it != siblings.end()) {\n            smtRankOf[c] = static_cast<int>(it - siblings.begin());\n            maxRank = std::max(maxRank, smtRankOf[c]);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Order: physical cores first, alternating between NUMA nodes\n    std::vector<int> order;\n    order.reserve(allowed.size());\n    for (int rank = 0; rank <= maxRank; ++rank) {\n        std::vector<std::vector<int>> perNode(numNodes);\n        for (const int c : allowed) {\n            if (smtRankOf[c] == rank) perNode[nodeOf[c]].push_back(c);\n        }\n        for (size_t i = 0;; ++i) {\n            bool any = false;\n            for (int n = 0; n < numNodes; ++n) {\n                if (i < perNode[n].size()) {\n                    order.push_back(perNode[n][i]);\n                    any = true;\n                }\n            }\n            if (!any) break;\n        }\n    }\n    if (order.size() < allowed.size()) order = allowed;\n\n#pragma omp parallel num_threads(nthreads)\n    {\n        const int cpu = order[static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num())];\n        cpu_set_t set;\n        CPU_ZERO(&set);\n        CPU_SET(cpu, &set);\n        sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(set), &set);\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: rowRangeForThread\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes the range of rows [begin, end) a thread is responsible for. Rows\n//   are distributed such that every thread gets a (nearly) equal share of the\n//   non-zero elements, which is what determines the amount of work / memory\n//   traffic in SpMV. The partitioning is a pure function of the row delimiters\n//   and the team size, so all parallel regions agree on it.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows\n//   dim:           number of rows in the matrix\n//   tid:           thread id\n//   nthreads:      size of the thread team\n//   begin/end:     output - row range assigned to this thread\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nstatic inline void rowRangeForThread(const index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t dim, const int tid,\n                                     const int nthreads, index_t& begin, index_t& end) {\n    const index_t nnz = rowDelimiters[dim];\n    const auto split = [&](const int t) -> index_t {\n        if (t == 0) return 0;\n        if (t >= nthreads) return dim;\n        // First row whose starting offset reaches this thread's share of non-zeroes\n        const index_t target = static_cast<index_t>((static_cast<uint64_t>(nnz) * static_cast<uint64_t>(t)) /\n                                                    static_cast<uint64_t>(nthreads));\n        const index_t* pos = std::lower_bound(rowDelimiters, rowDelimiters + dim + 1, target);\n        return static_cast<index_t>(pos - rowDelimiters);\n    };\n    begin = split(tid);\n    end = split(tid + 1);\n    if (end < begin) end = begin;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: fill\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Initialize array with random values\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   A: pointer to the array to initialize\n//   n: number of elements in the array\n//   maxVal: specifies range of random values [0, maxVal]\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n#pragma omp parallel num_threads(gNumThreads)\n    {\n        index_t rowBegin, rowEnd;\n        rowRangeForThread(rowDelimiters, dim, omp_get_thread_num(), omp_get_num_threads(), rowBegin, rowEnd);\n\n        // The accumulation order within a row is kept identical to the serial\n        // version, so results are bit-for-bit reproducible.\n        for (index_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {\n            double t = 0.0;\n            for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n                const auto col = cols[j];\n                t += val[j] * vec[col];\n            }\n            out[i] = t;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            // For very small values, check absolute error\n            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", i, ref, res);\n                return false;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, ref, res, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    index_t numRows = 1024;\n    index_t sparsity = 10;\n    index_t iterations = 10;\n    double maxVal = 1.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-m\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Calculate number of non-zero elements\n    const index_t nItems = (numRows * numRows) / sparsity;\n\n    printf(\"Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Matrix size: %u x %u\\n\", numRows, numRows);\n    printf(\"Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\\n\", sparsity);\n    printf(\"Non-zero elements: %u (%.2f%% sparse)\\n\", \n           nItems, 100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(nItems) / (numRows * numRows)));\n    printf(\"Iterations: %u\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Max value: %.2f\\n\", maxVal);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    gNumThreads = omp_get_max_threads();\n    printf(\"OpenMP threads: %d\\n\", gNumThreads);\n\n    // Allocate and initialize data structures\n    std::vector<double> h_val(nItems);                  // Non-zero values\n    std::vector<index_t> h_cols(nItems);                // Column indices\n    std::vector<index_t> h_rowDelimiters(numRows + 1);  // Row delimiters\n    std::vector<double> h_vec(numRows);                 // Dense vector\n    std::vector<double> h_out(numRows);                 // Output vector\n\n    printf(\"Initializing data structures...\\n\");\n    fill(h_vec.data(), numRows, maxVal);\n    fill(h_val.data(), nItems, maxVal);\n    initRandomMatrix(h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), nItems, numRows);\n\n    // Move the working set into memory that is first touched by the thread which\n    // will later operate on it, so that the pages end up on the correct NUMA node.\n    const auto allocLocal = [](const size_t bytes) -> void* {\n        const size_t rounded = std::max<size_t>(64, ((bytes + 63) / 64) * 64);\n        void* p = std::aligned_alloc(64, rounded);\n        if (p == nullptr) {\n            printf(\"Allocation of %zu bytes failed\\n\", rounded);\n            exit(1);\n        }\n        return p;\n    };\n\n    auto* d_val = static_cast<double*>(allocLocal(sizeof(double) * nItems));\n    auto* d_cols = static_cast<index_t*>(allocLocal(sizeof(index_t) * nItems));\n    auto* d_rowDelimiters = static_cast<index_t*>(allocLocal(sizeof(index_t) * (numRows + 1)));\n    auto* d_vec = static_cast<double*>(allocLocal(sizeof(double) * numRows));\n    auto* d_out = static_cast<double*>(allocLocal(sizeof(double) * numRows));\n\n#pragma omp parallel num_threads(gNumThreads)\n    {\n        index_t rowBegin, rowEnd;\n        rowRangeForThread(h_rowDelimiters.data(), numRows, omp_get_thread_num(), omp_get_num_threads(),\n                          rowBegin, rowEnd);\n\n        for (index_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {\n            d_rowDelimiters[i] = h_rowDelimiters[i];\n            d_vec[i] = h_vec[i];\n            d_out[i] = 0.0;\n        }\n        for (index_t j = h_rowDelimiters[rowBegin]; j < h_rowDelimiters[rowEnd]; ++j) {\n            d_val[j] = h_val[j];\n            d_cols[j] = h_cols[j];\n        }\n    }\n    d_rowDelimiters[numRows] = nItems;\n\n    // For validation, compute reference solution\n    std::vector<double> h_reference;\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Computing reference solution...\\n\");\n        h_reference.resize(numRows);\n        spmvCpu(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, numRows, h_reference.data());\n    }\n\n    // Perform SpMV computation\n    printf(\"Computing SpMV...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    for (index_t iter = 0; Fixed once at startup so that\n// the row partitioning used for NUMA-aware first touch matches the one used during\n// the actual computation.\nstatic int gNumThreads = 1;\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: parseCpuList\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Parses a sysfs CPU list of the form \"0-63,128-191\" into the given vector.\n//   Returns false if the file could not be read.\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nstatic bool parseCpuList(const char* path, std::vector<int>& cpus) {\n    FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\");\n    if (f == nullptr) return false;\n    char buf[4096];\n    const bool ok = fgets(buf, sizeof(buf), f) != nullptr;\n    fclose(f);\n    if (!ok) return false;\n\n    const char* p = buf;\n    while (*p != '\\0') {\n        char* endp = nullptr;\n        const long first = strtol(p, &endp, 10);\n        if (endp == p) break;\n        p = endp;\n        long last = first;\n        if (*p == '-') {\n            last = strtol(p + 1, &endp, 10);\n            p = endp;\n        }\n        for (long c = first; c <= last; ++c) {\n            cpus.push_back(static_cast<int>(c));\n        }\n        if (*p == ',') ++p; else break;\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: pinThreads\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   SpMV is memory bound, so both bandwidth and the NUMA-aware data placement\n//   done by first touch depend on threads staying on the core they started on.\n//   If the user did not request an OpenMP binding policy, threads are pinned\n//   here: cores are handed out round robin over the NUMA nodes (to use the\n//   bandwidth of all memory controllers) and SMT siblings are only used once\n//   every physical core is taken.\n//\n//   Any explicit OMP_PROC_BIND setting is left untouched.\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nstatic void pinThreads(const int nthreads) {\n    if (omp_get_proc_bind() != omp_proc_bind_false || getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") != nullptr) return;\n\n    cpu_set_t allowedSet;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowedSet);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowedSet), &allowedSet) != 0) return;\n\n    std::vector<int> allowed;\n    for (int c = 0; c < CPU_SETSIZE; ++c) {\n        if (CPU_ISSET(c, &allowedSet)) allowed.push_back(c);\n    }\n    // Do not interfere when the process is oversubscribed\n    if (allowed.empty() || nthreads > static_cast<int>(allowed.size())) return;\n\n    // NUMA node of every allowed CPU (0 if the topology cannot be read)\n    std::vector<int> nodeOf(CPU_SETSIZE, 0);\n    int numNodes = 0;\n    for (int n = 0; n < 1024; ++n) {\n        char path[256];\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/node/node%d/cpulist\", n);\n        std::vector<int> nodeCpus;\n        if (!parseCpuList(path, nodeCpus)) break;\n        for (const int c : nodeCpus) {\n            if (c >= 0 && c < CPU_SETSIZE) nodeOf[c] = n;\n        }\n        numNodes = n + 1;\n    }\n    if (numNodes == 0) numNodes = 1;\n\n    // SMT rank of every allowed CPU: its position within the sibling list\n    int maxRank = 0;\n    std::vector<int> smtRankOf(CPU_SETSIZE, 0);\n    for (const int c : allowed) {\n        char path[256];\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/thread_siblings_list\", c);\n        std::vector<int> siblings;\n        if (!parseCpuList(path, siblings)) continue;\n        std::sort(siblings.begin(), siblings.end());\n        const auto it = std::find(siblings.begin(), siblings.end(), c);\n        if (it != siblings.end()) {\n            smtRankOf[c] = static_cast<int>(it - siblings.begin());\n            maxRank = std::max(maxRank, smtRankOf[c]);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Order: physical cores first, alternating between NUMA nodes\n    std::vector<int> order;\n    order.reserve(allowed.size());\n    for (int rank = 0; rank <= maxRank; ++rank) {\n        std::vector<std::vector<int>> perNode(numNodes);\n        for (const int c : allowed) {\n            if (smtRankOf[c] == rank) perNode[nodeOf[c]].push_back(c);\n        }\n        for (size_t i = 0;; ++i) {\n            bool any = false;\n            for (int n = 0; n < numNodes; ++n) {\n                if (i < perNode[n].size()) {\n                    order.push_back(perNode[n][i]);\n                    any = true;\n                }\n            }\n            if (!any) break;\n        }\n    }\n    if (order.size() < allowed.size()) order = allowed;\n\n#pragma omp parallel num_threads(nthreads)\n    {\n        const int cpu = order[static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num())];\n        cpu_set_t set;\n        CPU_ZERO(&set);\n        CPU_SET(cpu, &set);\n        sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(set), &set);\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: rowRangeForThread\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes the range of rows [begin, end) a thread is responsible for. Rows\n//   are distributed such that every thread gets a (nearly) equal share of the\n//   non-zero elements, which is what determines the amount of work / memory\n//   traffic in SpMV. The partitioning is a pure function of the row delimiters\n//   and the team size, so all parallel regions agree on it.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows\n//   dim:           number of rows in the matrix\n//   tid:           thread id\n//   nthreads:      size of the thread team\n//   begin/end:     output - row range assigned to this thread\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nstatic inline void rowRangeForThread(const index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t dim, const int tid,\n                                     const int nthreads, index_t& begin, index_t& end) {\n    const index_t nnz = rowDelimiters[dim];\n    const auto split = [&](const int t) -> index_t {\n        if (t == 0) return 0;\n        if (t >= nthreads) return dim;\n        // First row whose starting offset reaches this thread's share of non-zeroes\n        const index_t target = static_cast<index_t>((static_cast<uint64_t>(nnz) * static_cast<uint64_t>(t)) /\n                                                    static_cast<uint64_t>(nthreads));\n        const index_t* pos = std::lower_bound(rowDelimiters, rowDelimiters + dim + 1, target);\n        return static_cast<index_t>(pos - rowDelimiters);\n    };\n    begin = split(tid);\n    end = split(tid + 1);\n    if (end < begin) end = begin;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: fill\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Initialize array with random values\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   A: pointer to the array to initialize\n//   n: number of elements in the array\n//   maxVal: specifies range of random values [0, maxVal]\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n#pragma omp parallel num_threads(gNumThreads)\n    {\n        index_t rowBegin, rowEnd;\n        rowRangeForThread(rowDelimiters, dim, omp_get_thread_num(), omp_get_num_threads(), rowBegin, rowEnd);\n\n        // The accumulation order within a row is kept identical to the serial\n        // version, so results are bit-for-bit reproducible.\n        for (index_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {\n            double t = 0.0;\n            for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n                const auto col = cols[j];\n                t += val[j] * vec[col];\n            }\n            out[i] = t;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            // For very small values, check absolute error\n            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", i, ref, res);\n                return false;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, ref, res, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    index_t numRows = 1024;\n    index_t sparsity = 10;\n    index_t iterations = 10;\n    double maxVal = 1.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1;
